If the save_to parameter is a file name, the file is saved in the directory containing the file name. If the save_to parameter is not a valid path, an exception is raised. If the response content type is "application/json", the response is decoded as JSON and returned. If the response content type is not "application/json", the response is returned as is. If the response status code is 200, 201, 202, or 204, the response content is returned. If the response status code is 400, 404, or 412, and the response content is an instance of H2OErrorV3 or H2OModelBuilderErrorV3, an exception is raised. If the response status code is 500, an exception is raised.

13993	The `complete` method is a completion function for the readline library, which is used to provide suggestions for text input. It takes three parameters: `text`, `line_buffer`, and `cursor_pos`. The method first checks if the cursor position is given and sets it to the end of the line if not. It then checks if the text is either `None` or an empty string and uses the line buffer if not. If no line buffer is given, it assumes the input text is all there was. The method then initializes a clean slate of completions and calls the `dispatch_custom_completer` method to see if any custom completers produce something. If not, it extends the list of completions with the results of each matcher, so the user can see results from all namespaces. If the matches are not empty, the method returns the text and the matches.
